- **21 Oct 2026, P0.5-04 (session 9, Sonnet), merged (PR 140, 5ae0287): the Weekly Care Report.** After merge: the migration was applied before merging and read back (`care_report_inputs` returns the counts above on the live project); the `api` function answers after the deploy. The first reports in the new format go out Monday 08:00 local; CI took three rounds (the pgTAP file did not allow for the one Google change the fixtures already hold). Sent Monday 08:00 local (was 09:00), kept in Insights. Kabsi's own counts come only from the audit log, in one function, `public.care_report_inputs(location, from, to, tz)` (service role): replies prepared (`reply_drafted`), replies, posts, photos, hours changes and profile edits published (`publication` events `live` or `in_review`, each publication once), photo batches (one per local day), profile checks (`google_check`), and changes found, kept and put back (`protection_change` `awaiting_review`, `accepted`, `corrected`). It also returns what needs the owner (open tasks, urgent first, three at most, the Coach's gaps left out) and the Growth agent's plan (the Coach's open gaps, three at most, only on a plan with the checklist). `_shared/care-report/compose.ts` words them without changing a count: the headline "Kabsi took care of N things this week" (a reply prepared and published is one thing), the Google Profile line, the time saved (5 minutes a reply published, 15 an update, 10 a photo batch, 10 a profile check that found something; "About 1 hour 20 minutes", always called an estimate, never money, the formula one tap away), the week's customer activity against the week before (Google's numbers through the Google layer; labelled Example figures while Google is in mock mode, which is every business until Gate A) and, on Pro, how people found you (the last full month's top five terms; examples in mock mode). Google's numbers sit in the report copy for 30 days and `public.clear_old_report_numbers()` (run at the end of the weekly job) removes `activity` and `terms` from older copies; Kabsi's counts stay; `retention.ts` says so. The email (`report-body.ts`, preview regenerated) leads with the headline and carries the profile, content, activity, time saved, what needs you and the plan lines; the report page (`care-blocks.tsx`) shows the same with the formula in a details block and an Example label on mock figures. No review text is read into the report. Migration (applied by the session, additive, nothing dropped): `care_report` (two functions). **Done-when check, pasted:** on the last 30 days, `care_report_inputs` and a hand-written count on `audit_events` agree. Harbour Lane Coffee (demo): function `{profile_checks 4330, replies_prepared 1, replies_published 1, posts_published 0, changes_found 0}`, SQL `{profile_checks 4330, replies_prepared 1, replies_published 1, posts_published 0, changes_found 0}`. - **21 Oct 2026, P0.5-03 part B (session 9, Sonnet): the holiday hours concierge.** Five days before a verified public holiday of the business's country Kabsi opens one question (a `hours_confirm` task, `holiday:<prompt>`) and emails it from the existing 5 minute `detect_tasks` job: subject `<Business>: your hours for <holiday>`, one yellow button "Choose your hours", three plain links (Normal hours, Closed, Custom hours; Ramadan has Normal hours and Different hours), category `approval_needed` (relabelled "Posts, photos and hours to approve"). The page `/h/<token>` and the Hours screen share one form: pick an answer, read what Google will show, Confirm. The answer is the approval: `closed` and `custom` insert one `special_hours` period and call `approve_publication('special_hours', ...)` (channel dashboard or email_link, 10 second undo, publish job), `normal` records the answer and sends nothing; a custom time is read back first and refused when closing is not after opening; a moon holiday can move 3 days either way; a manager needs the owner's `hours` approval policy; "closed" and "custom" by email link need the signed-in member (K-17), "normal" only the link; a second answer gets the first result; the undo gives the question back. Special hours the owner already set for those days count as the answer. Migrations (applied by the session): `holiday_calendar` (tables `holidays`, `holiday_prompts`, `private.detect_holidays` replacing the stub, `private.holiday_answer`, `answer_holiday_as`, `use_holiday_link`, `holiday_link_view`, `holiday_prompts_to_send`, `mark_holiday_prompt_sent`, `holiday_calendar`), `holiday_calendar_seed` (62 rows), `holiday_undo`; the cascade foreign keys of `holiday_prompts` applied with `execute_sql` and read back in the same call (R-44): 3 constraints (`holiday_prompts_holiday_id_fkey`, `holiday_prompts_location_id_fkey`, `holiday_prompts_special_hours_id_fkey`). - **21 Oct 2026, P0.5-03 part A (session 9, Sonnet): one Hours editor, and regular hours through the one pipeline.** The card is split in two pull requests (plan 2.5): part A the editor, part B the holiday concierge. `/app/hours` now has three modes and a calendar: **Opening hours** (each day open or closed, one to three periods, closing after midnight allowed, prefilled from the approved fact or the display line Google Protection stored), **Special days** (the existing form) and **Closed for a while** (first day closed and the reopening date; Google has no reopening date, so the days between are closed special hours), and a month calendar with the special days. - **21 Oct 2026, P0.4-10 merged (session 6 of the bundled plan, PR 116, 30c0186; Deploy passed; session 6 is finished, next is session 7): lifecycle emails driven by events.** Built on the engine and the template, and switched off until Hussein turns it on (`app_settings.lifecycle_emails`, with the moment of switching in `lifecycle_since`: nothing from before it is ever sent). Each email is due by a rule over a snapshot of the business (`public.lifecycle_snapshots`, counts from the audit log only): welcome with the one next step; reminders 1, 24 and 72 hours after access was asked for (they stop at access); a thank-you after the first approved reply; day 2 what Kabsi has done; day 7 your week with the yearly offer ($190 a year, two months free); day 11 three days left with both prices; day 14 trial ended and what continues in grace; a note about what is still waiting 17 and 30 days after the trial ended. Each goes once (the engine's dedupe key `life:<step>:<business>`), is never sent late (each step has a window), and follows the P0.4-06 trial rules (trial mail only while the entitlements state is trial; none for concierge, demo, partner-covered or paying businesses). Win-back is marketing: it needs the owner's own yes in every country today, and always a yes in the UK, EU and EEA, Switzerland, the UAE, Saudi Arabia and wherever the country is unknown. The job rides on the `notify_due` clock. No end-of-trial discount. No Google, Places or Anthropic call.

Email links last 72 hours (a database trigger caps every link), work once and belong to one item and one recipient; a second POST gets the first result (`action_tokens.result`). Opening a link reads only `public.action_link_view`, which is STABLE, so Postgres refuses any write in it. `public.use_action_link` does the link's own action: review replies and skips from the link alone (K-17); profile changes, hours and urgent reviews only for the recipient signed in on the page, which asks to sign in and comes back; another account's session is refused. After Approve the page shows "Publishing in 10 seconds" with Undo, which cancels that link's publication. Every use writes an `email_link_used` audit event with the user, IP country and user agent, and skips and Protection decisions made through a link now carry the channel `email_link`. CI green (1022 database tests on a fresh stack); migration applied by the session after CI and read back (10 function bodies match). Google stays in mock mode. Next in Wave P0.2: P0.2-06.
- **10 Oct 2026, P0.2-04 merged (PR 76, fa04fad; Deploy passed, `api` 67, `content` 60; mock end to end on QA Bakery passed). Open: the 25 Oct check and Hussein's 390 px look.** Branch claude/h-p0-2-04: Google Protection runs on Business Knowledge and Google's update flow.** The detector (`_shared/protection.ts`, replacing `_shared/shield.ts`) reads what Google shows for name, phone, website, address, regular hours, main category, open status and map pin, plus Google's own updates (`hasGoogleUpdated`, then `getGoogleUpdated` diffMask), compares them with `knowledge_facts` and records `profile_changes` through `record_protection_check`, with the explanation ("Google shows Sunday closing at 18:00. You approved 22:00.") and the K-77 name check. "Google is right" updates the fact and sends nothing; "Keep my information" re-sends the approved fact through the pipeline and counts as done only when Google shows it and drops it from diffMask. Guards in `decide_profile_change`: high-risk fields need the signed-in owner, 7 days apart; a third put-back in 30 days becomes a `google_support` staff follow-up. `listing_baselines` and `listing_changes` are retired (no code reads or writes them; nothing dropped) and `kabsi_profile_daily` blanks Google's values in them 30 days after they were read, so the two `system` baselines (Yawmiyati, QA Bakery) are blanked by the run of 25 Oct with no manual step. Nothing needs the blocked `mybusiness.googleapis.com`. Google stays in mock mode. Next in Wave P0.2: P0.2-05.

The Protection baselines arrive as `needs_confirmation`, source google, never as approved (A2); `confirm_business_details` lets the signed-in owner confirm the K-18 details as verified owner facts. `profile_changes` (K-20) mirrors `listing_changes` until P0.2-04 moves the detector; a newer change supersedes the open one in the database as `superseded`, never `kept` (A5); `kabsi_profile_daily` expires changes after 14 days and clears Google's values after 30 (K-40). No Google call; nothing needs the blocked `mybusiness.googleapis.com`. CI green (880 database tests on a fresh database). **P0.6-05 After merge: passed 21 Oct 2026, 19:20 to 19:26 UTC (see Evidence). Still to do: enforce the CSP on or after 4 Nov 2026 (14 clean days): `Content-Security-Policy` with per-request nonces instead of `'unsafe-inline'` for scripts (G-15), as its own follow-up card.**

**Session 11, for Rashid (dashboard steps only Rashid can read or do).**
1. Vercel, project kabsi-canvas, Firewall: Bot Protection should read **On, Log**. Set by this session with the connector; reading it back was refused by the session's permission check. Click path: vercel.com, itskaysyoga-5542s-projects, kabsi-canvas, Firewall, Configure, Bot Protection. After a week of logs, move it to Challenge if nothing real is caught (uptime checks and Meta's crawler are verified bots).
2. Supabase Auth Captcha (step 5 in the steps table): Supabase dashboard, project ynjdqjlmdwjgbfezevxy, Authentication, Attack Protection (or Settings, Bot and Abuse Protection), Enable Captcha protection, provider Turnstile, paste the Turnstile **secret** key (Cloudflare dashboard, Turnstile, the kabsi.co widget, Settings; the same key is already in the Edge Function secrets as TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY, which cannot be read back), Save. Read back: on kabsi.co/login send yourself one email code; it must arrive. If it fails with a captcha error, switch it off again and tell the next chat (the `auth-code` function already checks Turnstile itself).
3. On a phone (iOS Safari): kabsi.co/pricing shows the privacy banner at the foot; tap Choose, switch Statistics off, Save choices; "Privacy choices" in the footer reopens it.

**P0.6-02 After merge (session 11, 21 Oct 2026, 19:13 to 19:22 UTC).** 1 and 2 passed: `kabsi.co/ingest/static/array.js` 200; a POST to `kabsi.co/ingest/e/` gets PostHog's `{"status":"Ok"}` after Vercel's one trailing-slash 307; with posthog.com blocked a browser on kabsi.co sends its events to `kabsi.co/ingest` only; PostHog's events table shows `landing_viewed` (page_type home) arriving from kabsi.co and the test events sent through the proxy. 2. Make one graphic for real (the render itself needs a signed-in Pro user, so the session could not run it live; the deployed function answers 401 to an anonymous call, which proves it starts): sign in as Yawmiyati (a paid business), Reviews, open a 4 or 5 star review that has text, Make a graphic. Expect the square at once, then Story, then Download. If it fails, tell the next chat the message shown and look at the Edge Function logs for `review-graphic`.
3. On a phone (iOS Safari and Android Chrome, which the session could not test): Get reviews, "Send review link", WhatsApp should open with "Thanks for visiting ... here is our link: ...". Also on Get reviews switch one test link to "Show the page" and scan or tap it: the Kabsi page with the Google button first should open; switch it back to "Go straight to Google" when done. Add a WhatsApp number in Settings, About your business ("WhatsApp number for your review link page", with the country code) to see the manager line.

## Evidence

### P0.6-05 After merge (21 Oct 2026, 19:20 to 19:26 UTC, Rashid's session 11)
- Deploy run 39660852728 for 327764b: success (`assistant` and `review-link` deployed).
- `curl -sI https://kabsi.co`: `strict-transport-security: max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload`, `x-content-type-options: nosniff`, `referrer-policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `permissions-policy: camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()`, `content-security-policy-report-only: default-src 'self'; ...`. `curl -sI https://kabsi.co/app`: `cache-control: no-store`. The PR's Vercel preview showed the same headers first.
- CSP report: an image from example.com added on kabsi.co/about; the browser logged "[Report Only] Refused to load the image" and POSTed to Sentry's security endpoint; Sentry `kabsi-web` shows issue KABSI-WEB-6 "Blocked 'image' from 'example.com'" (culprit `img-src`).
- `review-link` burst from one address (15 calls through pg_net, no token): 10 x 400 `challenge_failed`, then 5 x 429 `rate_limited`.
- Nora: a new conversation from a visitor who is not signed in, with no token: 400 `challenge_failed` "Please complete the check below and send your message again." (refused before any model call). No live chat message was sent (Anthropic calls stay off, R-43).

### P0.6-05 (branch claude/p0-6-05, 21 Oct 2026, Rashid's session 11 of the bundled plan, Opus)
- `vercel.json`: one `/(.*)` rule with HSTS (max-age 63072000, includeSubDomains, preload), nosniff, `strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()`, and `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` (self; scripts from Sentry's CDN, Turnstile and Meta; the Supabase project over https and wss; Sentry ingest; Turnstile frames; `frame-ancestors 'none'`, `base-uri 'self'`, `form-action 'self'`, `object-src 'none'`; `report-uri` to Sentry project `kabsi-web`, id 4512145411801168, read from the loader script). PostHog needs no entry: it is same-origin through `/ingest`. `'unsafe-inline'` stays for scripts and styles while report-only (the boot script and the hydration data); nonces come with enforcement. `Cache-Control: no-store` on `/app`, `/staff`, `/start`, `/login`, `/a/*`, `/activate/*`, `/api/*`.
- Turnstile with a server check: sign-up and login by email code (`auth-code`, already), the Profile Check (`profile-snapshot`, already), the review link tool (`review-link`, already), the contact form (`lead`, already; no page sends it yet), and now Nora: `assistant` refuses a new conversation from a visitor who is not signed in without a valid token (`challenge_failed`, 400); the widget shows the Cloudflare check until a conversation exists and sends the token once. Live before this change: a call with no token to `profile-snapshot`, `review-link` and `lead` answers 400 `challenge_failed` (the secret is set). The partner form is after launch (R-42). Google sign-in has no form to protect.
- Rate limits: `review-link` now 10 a minute per IP before anything else (plus 20 an hour and 400 a day as before); `profile-snapshot` already 10 a minute, checked before Turnstile. Live burst on `profile-snapshot` from one address (pg_net, 15 calls): 10 x 400 then 5 x 429. From this container the 15 calls spread over 6 rate-limit keys because the outbound address rotates; a forged `X-Forwarded-For` is not trusted (no key change).
- Vercel Firewall (the edge in front of kabsi.co; Cloudflare only holds DNS, so its WAF and bot rules do not see kabsi.co traffic): Bot Protection managed ruleset switched on in **log** mode with the Vercel connector (the update returned success; reading the config back was refused by this session's permission check, so Rashid reads it back). Log first so uptime checks and the session's own curl checks are not challenged; switch to challenge after a week of logs.
- Tests: `tests/security-headers.test.ts` (7). Checks: typecheck, eslint and prettier on the changed files, `npm test` (33 files, 199 tests), `npm run build`, `deno check` on `assistant` and `review-link`. Screenshot at 390 px of Nora's panel with the Cloudflare check under the starters (looked at in the session, not committed).

### P0.6-02 (branch claude/p0-6-02, 21 Oct 2026, Rashid's session 11 of the bundled plan, Opus)
- Server events (`supabase/migrations/20261021234210_product_events.sql`, fix `20261021234220_product_events_fix.sql` and grants `20261021234230_product_events_grants.sql` (the first CI run failed `rls_functions.sql` tests 1 and 12: five new private functions were executable by public, and `save_signup_attribution` had no stranger test; both fixed, the stranger test added), all applied with `apply_migration`): `public.product_events` outbox, one row per source fact (`source_key` unique). Triggers: `audit_events` (google_access_granted with route and minutes_since_signup; reply_draft_ready with rating and risk; reply_approved with edited, seconds_to_approve and channel; reply_published, post_published, photo_published; profile_change_decided with field and decision; details_confirmed; backlog_cleared), `auth.users` (registration_completed with method google or email), `subscriptions` (trial_converted, payment_failed, subscription_cancelled). Demo businesses and kabsi.co accounts send nothing (`events_internal` = skip). Every trigger swallows its own errors so an analytics fault never stops the action. `private.send_product_events()` every minute (`kabsi_product_events`), 200 a batch, failed calls retried up to 5 times, the row id as PostHog's uuid.
- Browser (`src/lib/telemetry.ts`): `BrowserEvent` and `ServerEvent` name lists (exactly the card's 21 names); `track()` sends only browser names, and the pre-P0.6-02 names still called by some screens send nothing; landing_viewed with page_type, category and UTM fields on public pages only; business_found and snapshot_viewed (free check and onboarding), profile_state_seen, connect_started (route manual); `autocapture: false`; PostHog through `kabsi.co/ingest` (`vercel.json` rewrites to `us.i.posthog.com` and `us-assets.i.posthog.com`); replay stays public pages only and only after a yes (P0.6-01).
- Attribution (`src/lib/attribution.ts`, `public.save_signup_attribution`): first touch (also for a visit with no campaign), last touch (only with UTMs), fbclid and fbc only where advertising is allowed, fbp from Meta's cookie when it exists, `via=CODE` cookie `kabsi_via` 60 days, last wins; saved once right after the first sign-in for an account under a day old, checked and trimmed in SQL; the via code also goes to the user's businesses and to a business created later (trigger). `/privacy` section 7 and the facts file say so.
- Dry run on production inside a transaction that was rolled back (nothing kept, read back 0 users, 0 businesses, 0 events): a new email account, `save_signup_attribution`, `start_location`, access granted. Result: `registration_completed {method: email}` and `google_access_granted {route: manual, minutes_since_signup: 0}` once each; the attribution row with first and last touch, fbclid and fbc, the bad fbp refused, via `DANA7` on the account and on the business.
- Delivery: two test events (`test: p0602_check`, a made-up user id) sent by `private.send_product_events()`; pg_net 200 `{"status":"Ok"}` for both; PostHog's events table shows registration_completed (method email) and google_access_granted (route manual) once each. Then `product_events` stays on.
- Tests: `tests/events.test.ts` (7): name format, no shared name, exactly the card's list, the database sends only server names, track drops server and retired names, landing_viewed props and nothing on /app, first and last touch, via cookie Max-Age 60 days, no ad ids where advertising waits for a yes. `tests/telemetry-demo.test.ts` uses a browser event now. ### P0.8-07 (branch claude/h-p0-8-07, 21 Oct 2026, Hussein's session 8 of the bundled plan, Sonnet)
- What it is: **Make a graphic** on a 4 or 5 star review that still has words (the full card for a review that needs a reply, and the read-only view of the others). A bottom sheet offers a square (1080 x 1080) and a story (1080 x 1920), makes the square at once, shows the image, and offers Download and, where the phone allows it, Share. Kabsi posts nothing. A business that is not on Pro sees "Review graphics are part of Pro." with a link to Plan, and no image is made.
- Why Satori and resvg in an Edge Function (the card asked the chat to pick and record): an Edge Function keeps the Pro, role and rating checks on the server (a browser-only canvas could not enforce Pro), keeps the review text inside Kabsi's own database and the one request, and needs no new service. Everything the renderer needs is embedded (`_shared/review-graphic/assets.ts`: resvg-wasm gzipped, Readex Pro 400 and 700 and Lalezar 400 as woff, 1.3 MB), so it has no network or file dependency at run time. Satori reads woff, not woff2, so the fonts are the same OFL families the website serves, in woff, from `@fontsource`. Cost: none beyond the function call (no model, no paid API).
- Rules in code (`_shared/review-graphic/text.ts`): only 4 and 5 stars; first name and initial ("Maria K."; no name or a non-Latin one is "A customer"); links, bare domains, emails, phone-like digit runs, hashtags, handles and emoji removed; em and en dashes become a hyphen and exclamation marks become full stops (Kabsi's house style); cut at a space no earlier than character 120 and no later than 200, trailing punctuation dropped, then an ellipsis, so no word is broken. Arabic and other non-Latin text is refused with a plain message: Satori shapes Arabic letters but lays the words out in the wrong order (tested with a spike), so a wrong image is worse than none.
- Checks the function makes in order (`_shared/review-graphic/handler.ts`): signed in (401), owner or manager of the review's business (404 for staff, a stranger or a missing review, so existence is not revealed), 40 graphics an hour per person (429, fail closed), Pro through `public.feature_access` asked again on the server (403 `pro_required`), rating and words (422). Nothing is stored (no Storage, no table); the log line is `{ok, size}` only; review text and names are never logged.
- Done when: "a 4-star and a 5-star review produce both sizes and a 3-star one is refused": Deno `render.test.ts` (real PNGs: signature bytes and width and height 1080 x 1080 and 1080 x 1920 for 4 and 5 stars) and `text.test.ts` and `handler.test.ts` (a 3 star review gets 422 `rating` and nothing is rendered). "A 600-character review is cut at about 200 with an ellipsis and no mid-word break": `text.test.ts` (length at most 201, at least 150, ends with the ellipsis, the last word is whole). "A review with a phone number or link is cleaned": `text.test.ts` (numbers, emails, www, https, bare .lb and .com, hashtags and handles gone). "No image or review text remains in Storage after 30 days": nothing is stored; `tests/review-graphic.test.ts` fails if the function files touch `.storage`, `.insert`, `.upsert`, `.update`, `.delete` or `writeFile`; the retention job still clears review text at 30 days and a purged review gets no button (`graphicEligible`). "An Essentials business is told it is a Pro feature": `handler.test.ts` (403 and the message "Review graphics are part of Pro.", nothing rendered) and the sheet shows the same line before any call. Colours come from `TOKENS` only (a vitest check fails on a hex value in the renderer).
- Read: the square and the story for a 5 star short review and a 4 star 200 character review (first pass had the quote crowding the name in the square and a big gap in the story; the quote now sits in a centred block, sizes by length, re-read), and the sheet at 390 px in Chromium (Pro with the image, and not Pro). The Reviews list itself was not changed.
- Checks: `deno check` on every changed function and test; `deno test` 20 new tests pass here and the whole `_shared/` suite passes with `--allow-env` (323 tests); `scripts/deno-check.sh` now runs `_shared/` with `--allow-env` because Satori reads `NODE_ENV` and `JEST_WORKER_ID` when it loads. `npm run typecheck` clean; `npm test`; eslint clean; build.
- Facts: `knowledge/kabsi-facts.md` and `public/llms-full.txt` (the old [COMING] bullet is now a live paragraph). `docs/testing.md` says how `assets.ts` is made.
- Google guard: not applicable (nothing under `_shared/google/`, no Google call, nothing is posted). The review text used is Kabsi's own copy, within its 30 day limit, shown only to the owner who asked, as first name and initial with text cut to about 200 characters (K-113). No model or paid API call.
- Found, not done: Arabic and other non-Latin reviews get no graphic until the layout handles right to left text (a later pass could use the Arabic font and a bidi step). The Deploy of this function could not be run here; After merge checks it.

### H-TANSTACK-XSS security hotfix (branch claude/h-hotfix-tanstack-xss, 8 Oct 2026, Hussein's session)
- Trigger: Vercel build `bld_b0dq1cf03` of 869c0d8 (deployment dpl_9RCakgc6Uu4VsZfFz5zJYjhu9L2a, state ERROR) printed "@tanstack/react-start@1.168.32 contains a known cross-site scripting vulnerability (CVE-2026-102989). Update to 1.168.60 or later and redeploy." `DANGEROUSLY_DEPLOY_VULNERABLE_TANSTACK_START_XSS` was not set; no Vercel setting was changed.
- Versions: 1.168.60 is the first release at or above the fixed version (and the latest on npm, published 30 Sep 2026, past the 24h `minimumReleaseAge`). It pins `@tanstack/react-router` 1.170.41, and its `start-plugin-core` pins `@tanstack/router-plugin` 1.168.42, so the three direct pins moved together. `npm ls` shows one copy of each (the Lovable config's peer dedupes to 1.168.60).
- `bun.lock` regenerated with `bun install --lockfile-only` against npm (Lovable builds from it); only the TanStack tree and its own dependencies changed, plus `vitest`, which `package.json` already listed but the old lockfile lacked. The 8 Lovable-registry tarball URLs are unchanged.
- Breaking change fixed: the router's `ErrorComponentProps.error` is now `unknown`; `src/routes/__root.tsx` uses that type (both reporters already take `unknown`). The file was formatted with Prettier because `lint:changed` lints every touched file; no other code changed.
- Checks: `npm run typecheck` 0 errors; eslint on `__root.tsx` clean; `check:anon`, `check:tokens`, `check:google` ok; `npm test` 21 of 21 passed; `npm run build` passed. The built Worker run locally with `wrangler dev`: `/` 200 (title "Kabsi | Your reviews and listing. Taken care of."), `/pricing` 200, `/privacy` 200, `/login` 200, an unknown path 404, a JS asset 200.
- `npm audit` after the upgrade: 1 critical, 4 high, all build or dev tooling, none in the served bundle; not fixed here (see Found, not done).
- PR 52 CI on a539b2c: App, Edge Functions and Worker, Database all success; Vercel preview dpl_BJbsE28cKNtYVmnsFGnRbm5P5iEd Ready (`/pricing` 200, `x-robots-tag: noindex`). Merged 03:04 UTC as merge commit 70c1188.
- After merge: the Deploy workflow did not run for 70c1188, as designed (its `paths` cover only `supabase/functions/**`, `supabase/config.toml`, `workers/kabsi-go/**` and the workflow; none changed). Vercel production deployment dpl_DLD6VyX9wwq3gNUYpRfZ1NA6TKTZ for 70c1188 is READY on kabsi-canvas.vercel.app (unknown path 404 with the Kabsi not-found page); the CVE stop is gone. Vercel is not yet serving kabsi.co (P0.1-03).
- How the site is published today: kabsi-app.lovable.app is Lovable hosting of the "Kabsi Canvas" project (Lovable builds for Cloudflare Workers but publishes only on Publish in Lovable); kabsi.co still serves the old Lovable project "Kabsi". The Cloudflare Worker `kabsi-go` serves only go.kabsi.co.

- What the migration does: table `audit_events` (no foreign keys, so it outlives what it describes); `private.audit(...)` the only insert path; a trigger refuses UPDATE, DELETE and TRUNCATE for everyone outside `private.run_retention`, and inside it allows only blanking `before`/`after` and deleting rows older than 24 months; recording triggers on `publications` (approval on insert, publication on a final status), `locations` (knowledge edit with only the changed fields, onboarding step, consent, access granted or lost, Google check, concierge on or off, deletion requested or cancelled), `location_members` and `organization_members` (roles), `reviews` (draft ready, skipped, handled offline, concierge review entered) and `concierge_tasks`; `public.activity_feed(location, days)` for members and staff; `private.run_retention` keeps every step it had and adds the audit rules. Read RLS: members of the business, owners and admins of the organisation, staff. No review text or reviewer name goes into the log.
- Request context (K-17): `approve` and `action` send `x-kabsi-request-id`, `x-kabsi-ip-country`, `x-kabsi-user-agent` and `x-kabsi-actor-id` with the service-role writes (`_shared/audit.ts`); the database honours them only when the caller is the service role. A browser RPC records its own `user-agent` and `cf-ipcountry`.

### Deploy workflow fix and P0.1-02b part B After-merge checks (4 Oct 2026, Hussein's session)
- Deploy run 5 (37185310050, merge of PR 20, commit 2a204f9): Edge Functions job success, Worker kabsi-go job failure. Log line: "Missing entry-point: The entry-point should be specified via the command line ... or the `main` config field." The four runs on 29 Sep had the same shape (Edge Functions success, Worker failure).
- Cause: `cloudflare/wrangler-action@v3` installs Wrangler 3.90.0 by default, which does not read `workers/kabsi-go/wrangler.jsonc`. Reproduced with `wrangler deploy --dry-run` on a clean copy of the folder: 3.90.0 fails with the same error; 3.114.17 bundles the Worker (3.41 KiB) and lists the STICKERS binding and vars.
- Fix: PR 21 pins `wranglerVersion: "3.114.17"` in `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` (merged as 7b82144). Deploy run 6 (37185695836) on 7b82144: both jobs success. This was the first Worker deploy from CI, so the Worker code on main is now live on go.kabsi.co; the TAP_SECRET set in the Cloudflare dashboard is untouched by `wrangler deploy`.
- Edge Function versions after run 6 (`list_edge_functions`): `api` 34, `content` 27, `assistant` 21, all updated 2026-10-04 about 07:25 UTC, after the PR 20 merge.
- Mock-review check on Yawmiyati (location 9803ee99-fee8-4c6a-abb4-1a830fcbb438, mock mode): `staff_mock_review` needs a staff login that the Supabase connector does not have, so the same row it writes was inserted into `mock_google_reviews` (review id mock-eafac0a35ce9446684800827a9285d6e, 3 stars, reviewer "Deploy check", 07:27:37 UTC). The cron synced it at 07:30:03 (reviews row e3852d23-d5ff-4253-829c-001e0f61df61, state drafted, one reply draft) and sent the email at 07:30:08: `emails` row 84558298-d9f7-4d72-8f19-02b99d59f869, kind review_new, status sent, subject "New review for Yawmiyati (3 of 5)", to rashid.hamzy@gmail.com, resend_id 01a105d1-f845-7ee8-b09d-3a13d23eb9db.
- Wording in the deployed code (`get_edge_function` for `api`, searched for strings): "Review reply" 2 hits, "Nothing is published until you approve it." 1, "trademarks of Google LLC" 1, "Kabsi is operated by Hussein Slim, Dubai, United Arab Emirates" 1; "Beirut, Lebanon", "only posts what you approve", "Put mine back" and ">Post<" 0 hits.
- Plan correction: the `emails` table has no body column (columns: id, location_id, partner_id, kind, to_address, subject, resend_id, status, error, dedupe_key, created_at). The plan's "check the `emails` row body" (P0.1-02b Done when, and the standard prompt) cannot be done. - P0.1-13b part A, test data on Yawmiyati (7 Oct): post 2234d0ec's `gbp_posts` row ("P0.1-13b after-merge check"), the 25 Dec 2026 special hours row, publications 2234d0ec, 758a9abf, 954b559a; the mock listing phone and `specialHours`. Listed under Test data to delete.
- P0.1-13b part A: Google Protection "keep" now refuses while a put-back is on its way (`reverting`); the owner must wait for it or undo it in 10 seconds. There is no Undo button for posts, photos, hours or put-backs yet (K-70 places the Undo button in P0.3, and the content routes have no `undo` action yet); the database undo works for every kind.
- P0.1-13a: if the owner replies on Google directly during the 10 to 70 seconds between approval and the publish job, Kabsi's reply replaces theirs (the sync does not touch a review in `publishing`). Same exposure as before the pipeline, now with a slightly longer window; a read of Google just before the write would close it (P0.1-13b or P0.7-01b).
- P0.1-13a: `concierge_queue_reply` is no longer called by any code (the claim makes the concierge task now); drop it in P0.1-13b's contract step.
- P0.1-12b: the per-business schedules (`next_sync_at`, `next_protection_at`) are set to "now plus 5 minutes" at the start of the minute the job is offered, and pg_cron's next tick often starts a few milliseconds earlier, so a business is offered every 5 or 6 minutes (seen live: 03:30, 03:35, 03:41). Harmless; rounding the next time down to the minute would make it exactly 5.

- P0.1-12b: until P0.1-13a moves publishing onto the queue, a live reply, post or listing change that cannot get its place under the limiter within 65 s (or meets an open breaker) fails with `GoogleBusy` and is left "publishing" for staff to verify (D266 treats it as uncertain, because a read-back after a successful write can also meet it). Mock mode is unaffected. P0.1-13a's publish job should postpone instead.
- P0.1-12b: `/api/cron-tick` and `shieldCheck()` stay after `kabsi_cron_tick` is unscheduled (the `weekly_now` test hook uses the route). A later contract step removes them.
- P0.1-12b: `ratings_snapshot` and `weekly_reports` still loop over every business inside one job. Once a day (ratings) and Mondays (reports) that is up to 500 Places calls or report builds in one run; a run cut off by the function limit is picked up by the next one (each step decides from data). One job per business belongs with P0.5-04 (reports) or P0.2-06 (Places cost guard).
- P0.1-12b: Places API calls (`places()`), the health check's `listAccountsOnce` and the token refresh do not pass the limiter: Places has its own quota (P0.2-06), the other two are single calls.
- P0.1-12b: K-36 and the audit's failure table say Home shows "Google is not responding. Your information is safe. We'll try again." while the breaker is open. Not built (screen work); P0.3-07.

- P0.1-12a merge: `deploy.yml` installed Supabase CLI "latest", and the first Deploy after the merge failed on GitHub's API rate limit (re-run passed). Fixed on Hussein's request by branch claude/h-deploy-pin-cli: `deploy.yml` pins 2.119.0 like `ci.yml`.

- P0.1-12a: `private.ops_watchdog` still alerts "Reviews job hasn't run for 20 minutes ... New reviews aren't being drafted" from `kabsi_cron_tick`; after this task drafting runs on `kabsi_dispatch`. P0.1-12b (which retires `kabsi_cron_tick`) should point the watchdog at the dispatcher. Done by P0.1-12b (migration 2 step 4).

If Google sends a string, no invitation would ever match on address and every invite would stay pending. Not changed (behaviour must not change in this task); check against a captured invitation on Gate A day (P0.7-01) and fix in `invitations.ts`.

- 21 Oct 2026, session 11, P0.6-05: (a) Cloudflare managed rules and bot protection cannot apply because kabsi.co is DNS-only on Cloudflare (plan P0.6-05 says so); Vercel's Bot Protection is the equivalent and is on in log mode, to move to challenge after a week of logs. (b) Nora asks for Turnstile once per conversation for visitors who are not signed in, not on every message. (c) No `X-Frame-Options` header: the card keeps framing rules inside the report-only CSP until enforcement.
- 21 Oct 2026, session 11: (a) P0.6-01 rules: unknown country counts as the strictest (opt_in); UK, UAE and Saudi Arabia share one rule (statistics in totals with an opt-out, advertising opt-in); before any choice PostHog runs in memory everywhere, as G-18 says. (b) P0.6-01 country: kabsi.co is DNS-only on Cloudflare, so `/api/geo` reads Vercel's `x-vercel-ip-country` (Cloudflare's header first if it is ever proxied). (c) P0.6-02 proxy: `kabsi.co/ingest` through Vercel rewrites instead of an `e.kabsi.co` Worker route: same effect, no DNS step. (d) P0.6-02: the card's touch text says 90 days; the K-127 change note above it says 60, so the via cookie is 60 days. (e) P0.6-02: kabsi.co accounts are treated as internal testers and send no events (`app_settings.events_internal` = skip; set it to `send` to include them).

The page's WhatsApp number is a new Business Knowledge field, "WhatsApp number for your review link page" (country code required, never used in replies). - 21 Oct 2026, planning chat (Hussein to confirm or change; each is a call the planning chat made to make a decision buildable): (1) **K-130 auto-fix versus Google's policy.** The audit found that Google's policy does not allow the API to revert Google's updates automatically (K-19). (K-112) and the non-affiliation notice stays in view. A Google brand check is worth doing before launch. (e) The customer activity line is the review-link opens of the last 30 days, labelled as activity, not reviews, because profile views and calls need Google performance data that is not read until Insights (P0.5-05).
- 15 Oct 2026, P0.3-06: (a) "Owner approves" is the default for every kind, so a manager approves nothing until the owner turns a kind on in Settings, Team (K-16 "Manager: whatever the owner's policy allows"). (b) Map pin joins name, address, main category and open status as owner-only (it was already a high-risk Protection field). (c) Skip and "I'll reply myself" follow the reply approval rule. (d) Approval emails go to owners only; managers approve in the app. (e) `approval_policies` is the one policy store; `partner_clients.approval_policy` (P0.1-09, no reader) stays unread until a contract step. (f) A manager may change Business Knowledge (as in P0.3-01), the review link, email settings and auto posts; billing, plan, consent, onboarding, disconnect and deletion are the owner's. (g) Staff land on a simple Home with Add photos and Share the review link.

- 15 Oct 2026, P0.3-05: (a) Detectors are SQL functions, run at once by statement triggers on the source tables and every 5 minutes by the `detect_tasks` job (the card's "job handlers"); a detector error in a trigger is logged and never blocks the owner's action. (b) One review task per review; urgent for high risk or a reply Google refused, otherwise recommended. Photos are one batch task per business. (c) The plan task appears 7 days before the paid time ends (urgent at 2); Home used to show it 30 days before, which for a monthly plan meant always. (d) Profile gaps keep the old score's items without points: six Business Knowledge keys (description, service, hours note, contact, signature, phrase to use), phone, website, main category, opening hours, the review link, 10 photos and a post in 14 days (the last two not for concierge businesses). (e) Later (3 days) and Skip (30 days) are both a snooze, for owner and manager; staff rights come with P0.3-06. (f) Tasks hold no review text, reviewer name or Google value, so K-40 needs no new rule. (g) `concierge_tasks` (staff work) stays a staff queue, not owner tasks.

- 14 Oct 2026, P0.3-04: (a) The gate lives in ci.yml (job "Prompt gate (evaluation set)") so the green-ci.yml merge rule enforces it; it fails closed while `EVAL_LIVE` is off rather than skipping, so no prompt change merges unevaluated. (b) The scoring uses its own, broader patterns (gifts, "on us", free items count as compensation) and scores only drafts that come out ready, because only those reach an owner. (c) Each case's `expect.risk` is the lowest level it may get; only high is exact. (d) Healthcare cases use a fictional dental practice (Google category Dentist).

- 14 Oct 2026, P0.3-03: (a) A high-risk review waits as state `blocked` (no new state), so every queue that already shows "needs you" shows it. (b) When the code rules already say high, Haiku is not asked: nothing can lower the level and there is no draft; the review's language stays unknown and a holding reply answers in the review's language. (c) Medium emails keep the "Review reply" button (the old urgent email had none): K-17 asks sign-in only for the high-risk class. (d) The high-risk email subject is "A review for <Business> needs a little more care" (R-30's subject is for a review with a reply ready). (e) The task touches about 17 code files, more than the plan's 10; Hussein asked for one pull request per task. (f) The reason sentences and the checklist wording are Kabsi's (K-13 gives only the refund example and the title).

- 13 Oct 2026, P0.3-02: (a) Prompts are versioned TypeScript files (`_shared/ai/prompts/reply.v1.ts` and so on), not `.md`, because the Edge Function bundle only carries imported modules. (b) `ai_runs` keeps no text (hashes, versions, check names, approver); the text is the draft in `reply_drafts`, already redacted with its review after 30 days, so nothing in `ai_runs` needs its own redaction. (c) The memory version is the hash of the memory text, which changes only when a fact, the voice, a rule or an open question changes; the last 5 approved replies are a separate cached part. (d) Kinds of edit noted in code: shorter, longer, adds the name, without the name, adds an apology, without an apology; each offered once per business, and a no is final. A fact the owner adds is offered once per sentence and, if saved, is added to "Things you'd like mentioned when relevant". (e) A capitalised word that the review also uses, or any mid-sentence capitalised word that is not in the facts, fails the name check (places too); the problem text is generic so it never stores review content. (f) Replies carry no exclamation marks (house rule D244), changed from "at most one".

- 13 Oct 2026, P0.3-01: (a) A fact past its `review_after` date is left out of drafts until the owner confirms it again ("Out of date"), rather than used while Kabsi asks; K-120 says unknown is better than invented. (b) While a two-answers question is open, that fact is left out of drafts even though the owner's value has the higher trust, so nothing is guessed. (c) "Customers see" (K-11) is read as hours notes, regular hours, phone, contact, services, products and prices; other disagreements are held without a question. (d) Owners and managers may change facts (the old card let any member); staff roles come with P0.3-06. (e) Profile details (`profile.*`, K-18) stay on `confirm_business_details` and are not on the About your business screen. (f) Until the P0.3-05 task engine, `knowledge_confirms` is the stub for the knowledge_confirm task and shows on About your business. (g) The card's sync trigger and the `knowledge_card` column stay (no writer is left); dropping them is a later contract step that asks Hussein. (h) Nora's `save_fact` tool now writes a verified owner fact through the same RPC.

- 12 Oct, P0.4-01: (1) Retention of the email request for the free check (address, Place ID, tips choice, words, page, time): no automatic deletion date is built, and /privacy and the facts say "until you ask us to remove it", like contact details given to the assistant. K-40 has no row for it; decide a period (for example 24 months, or 12 months after the last contact if the person never signs up) and I will add a `retention_policies` row. (2) Findings rules: Urgent is a closed status or missing hours; Recommended is no phone, no website, no photos, fewer than 10 reviews, plus "Replies to your reviews" always; Nice to have is "Fresh photos" and "Posts to keep your profile fresh" always, so every check lists 3 to 5 items, and the last three say plainly that Google's public data cannot show replies or photo dates (K-82). Skip and "I'll handle it" never need it, since nothing is published. - 10 Oct 2026, P0.2-04: (a) Watched fields are name, phone, website, address, regular hours, main category, open status and map pin (the card's list); service area, special hours, description, attributes and verification state (K-19's longer list) stay for P0.3-11. (c) Card keys map: about to description, services to service, price_notes to price_note, payment_methods to payment_method, languages to language, policies to policy, contact_phone to contact (kind phone, used nowhere public, K-113), mention to phrase_use, avoid to phrase_avoid, tone and tone_notes to voice (part tone, notes), signature_ar to signature (language ar), staff_names to one staff_member each, custom_rules to special_rule, faqs to faq; services stays one fact (splitting the text would be guessing). (f) A change Google keeps showing after it expired is not alerted again for the same value. (g) K-20 severity "urgent for hours" read as regular and special hours.
- 9 Oct 2026, P0.2-02: (a) "Cancels open tasks" read as: approvals still inside their undo window are cancelled (their item goes back as undo does), reviews that were new, drafted or blocked become `archived` (Kabsi does not act on them; they stay until K-40 clears them), open concierge tasks are cancelled, unused email links expire. Drafts of posts, photos and hours stay as the owner's own drafts, and open Google Protection changes stay open (no state says "cancelled" without inventing an owner decision); both stop mattering because nothing can be approved or sent while the business is paused, and K-40 clears the Google values 30 days after access ends. (d) Kabsi's admin entry is any entry whose account is one of the accounts Kabsi's login lists (live: Kabsi Clients, `accounts/113746201522792609010`); only MANAGER entries are removed, never an owner's. (e) **Confirmed by Hussein on 9 Oct (this chat):** the access-change notice on accepting the invitation replaces the old "Kabsi is connected" email (same kind `access_granted` and dedupe key, new subject "Kabsi is now a Manager of <business> on Google") rather than adding a second email; disconnect sends `access_change` emails (removing when staff must finish, removed when done). (f) 7 business days count Monday to Friday in UTC from the request, without public holidays. (g) Only the owner role may disconnect (not managers, partners or staff); a demo business cannot be disconnected (R-17). (h) After a disconnect the owner reconnects by writing to hello@kabsi.co; a self-serve reconnect is not built. (i) Two Slack wording fixes rode along in the re-created `ops_on_location` (house rules): no exclamation mark in the partner "is live" message, and the deletion line says "remove this business from Kabsi. It goes in 7 days".

- P0.1-13b part B: Home and Reviews list a publication once Google has answered (`published`, `verifying`, `checking`, `verified`). The old `sent` also covered the few seconds while the call is running (`publishing`); those are now left out until Google answers.
- P0.1-13b part B: the audit log keeps its result words (`live`, `in_review`, `rejected`, `failed`, `cancelled`) and the `status` key in before and after, mapped from `state` by `private.publication_status_of`, and adds a `state` key, so the "What Kabsi did" feed and older lines read the same.
(Build chats add decisions the plan did not cover here, one line each with the reason. The planning chat folds confirmed ones into the plan.)
- P0.1-13b: the migration was applied in two parts because the Supabase connector cannot run a statement containing `drop` (it waits for a confirmation the session cannot give): the functions through the connector, the constraint and index swap by Hussein in the SQL editor. The migration history therefore has `publication_pipeline_content_part2_functions` and no row for part 1 (the SQL editor writes none); add it to the migration-history follow-up.
- P0.1-13b: split into part A (paths on the pipeline, this PR) and part B (status readers to `state`, then the contract drop), because the card needs more than 10 code files and the drop may only follow merged code that no longer reads `status` (plan 2.3, 2.5).
- P0.1-13b: posts, photos, special hours and put-backs also wait 10 seconds before going to Google, like replies, so the one pipeline has one rule and an Undo can be added to every kind in P0.3.
- P0.1-13b: a post, photo or hours period Google refuses is marked failed as before (not returned to draft); a reply still goes back to the owner. A post can be drafted again; a photo or hours change Google refused rarely succeeds unchanged.
- P0.1-13b: a Protection put-back Google does not show yet stays "checking" (Google may hold profile edits for review) and is failed after 7 days; a post, photo or hours period Google does not show after an unanswered write is failed at the first check ("Kabsi did not send it again"), because those writes are not held for review.
- P0.1-13b: the photo's signed link is made by the publish job when it sends, not at approval; a missing file ends the publication as rejected with nothing sent.
- P0.1-13b: the special hours row is saved as a draft and approved in the same request; if the approval fails, the row is set to skipped so the dates can be tried again.
- P0.1-13a: `publications.status` stays and follows `state` through a trigger (both ways), so the post, photo, hours and Protection paths and the concierge staff functions work unchanged until P0.1-13b; the contract step drops `status` once nothing reads it.
- P0.1-13a: the pipeline functions are in `public` with execute for the service role only (the Edge Functions call them through PostgREST, as with `claim_jobs`); undo goes through `api/approve` and `api/action` (the email link that approved may undo for 10 seconds), not a browser RPC.
- P0.1-13a: the Undo button itself is not built (K-70 places it in P0.3); this task builds the 10 second window, the RPC and the routes. Until then a reply reaches Google 10 to 70 seconds after approval (the dispatcher runs every minute).
- P0.1-13a: the reconcile schedule is 10 minutes, then 30 minutes, 1, 3, 6, 12 and 24 hours, then daily; a reply Google still does not show 7 days after approval is failed and goes back to the owner (K-116.1 says only "widening").
- P0.1-13a: when reconcile finds a different reply on Google (the owner replied there directly), the publication is failed with "Google shows a different reply for this review. Kabsi did not post again."; the next sync marks the review handled by the owner.

- 7 Oct: step 4 now removes both of Rashid's addresses from Google Cloud and Search Console once the kabsi.co owners are confirmed (R-32: no personal address as owner or recovery contact).
- P0.1-12b: the limiter and breaker guard live Google calls only (`gbp`); mock calls never reach Google's quota, so they are not limited. The load test therefore measures the queue and the dispatcher, and the limiter is proven by its own tests.
- P0.1-12b: the per-profile limit counts every write to a profile (replies, posts, photos, listing edits), not only listing edits, so publishing stays under Google's 10 a minute whatever the mix.
- P0.1-12b: the breaker counts each failed try (429, 5xx, no answer); 4xx answers about one business do not count. While it is open, jobs are postponed to the end of the pause without counting a try.
- P0.1-12b: the task is two migrations: limiter first (before merge), the producers and the unschedule after the deploy, because the deployed dispatcher must know the new job kinds before they are offered.
- P0.1-12b: the dispatcher runs each batch of 5 jobs side by side (it ran them one by one), so 500 businesses (about 200 jobs a minute on the mock) fit inside one 40-second run.
- P0.1-12a: `claim_jobs`, `finish_job`, `enqueue_job` and `record_sync_result` are in `public` with execute for the service role only (the plan says `private.claim_jobs`): the dispatcher calls them through PostgREST, which serves only `public` (P0.1-08 rule). The producers and the tick are in `private`.
- P0.1-12a: the review sync stays every 5 minutes per business (the old cron rate), each business at a fixed offset inside the 5 minutes, so calls spread evenly; incremental sync by update time (K-35) is not part of this task.
- P0.1-12a: a draft that fails is retried by the job with backoff and counted on `reviews.draft_attempts` as before; at 3 the job ends `failed` and the review stays in the owner's inbox. An exhausted AI budget ends the job without a failure and the producer offers the review again (as the old loop did).
- P0.1-12a: Google refusing access (403 or 404) is not retried by the job; the old 30-minute grace and the access-lost email are kept, and the next sync 5 minutes later is the retry.
- P0.1-12a: finished jobs are cleared by the tick: succeeded after 7 days, failed and dead after 30 days (jobs hold no review text).
- P0.1-12a: Home shows the last check line only once a check has happened (concierge businesses are not read from Google). The full K-06 header strip is P0.3-07.
- P0.1-11: live and mock modules return Google's wire types and `index.ts` maps them, except the Google Protection listing in mock mode, which keeps its display strings in `mock_listings` (the staff mock edit screen writes them) and is read and put back as before.
- P0.1-11: the Places mock is used by tests only; Places has no mode switch at run time (it needs only `PLACES_API_KEY`), and demo businesses have no place id.
- P0.1-11: the verifications module is read only (Voice of Merchant state and the list); Kabsi never starts a verification (Google reality check).
- P0.1-11: part A is over the plan's "about 10 files" because the card itself asks for 13 areas with two files each; the call-site moves are kept for part B.
- P0.1-10: events are written by triggers on the tables every path already writes (publications, locations, members, reviews, concierge tasks) instead of explicit calls in each Edge Function, so no path can skip the log; the request context reaches them through `x-kabsi-*` headers that count only for the service role.
- P0.1-10: the log stores ids and states, not reply text or reviewer names; the feed reads the reviewer's first name from `reviews` at read time, so it disappears with the review's 30-day purge (K-40). The exact approved text stays in `publications` as before.
- P0.1-10: `audit_events` has no foreign keys (a cascade would be a refused DELETE); a deleted business's `before`/`after` are blanked by retention and the who, what and when stay 24 months.
- P0.1-10: "What Kabsi did" shows Google checks (one line a day), drafts ready and publications that went live or to Google's review; approvals, failed, rejected and cancelled publications are recorded but not shown as work done. Staff can read every business's events (support).
- P0.1-10: no backfill; the log starts when the migration is applied.
- P0.1-09: the new tables are kept in step with the old columns by triggers (schema `private`) until later tasks move the writers, so nothing reads stale data at any step; the triggers go in the contract step.
- P0.1-09: a business joins its creator's oldest owner organisation of the same kind (demo or real); a demo business never joins a real organisation. Location managers and staff are not organisation members; only owners are.
- P0.1-09: `organizations.partner_id` links a partner organisation to its old `partners` row until P1-01 moves the agency details; `partner_clients.status` is derived from the business status (active, paused, disabled to ended, everything else onboarding) while `locations.partner_id` is the source.
- P0.1-09: `google_connections.access_state` is none, pending, granted, error or lost, derived from the old columns; `kabsi_account`, `last_attempted_sync_at`, `next_sync_at`, `sync_status` and `last_error` stay empty until P0.1-11 and P0.1-12a write them.
- P0.1-09: `partner_clients.approval_policy` accepts only the five K-16 kinds (review_reply, post, photo, profile_change, hours) with owner or partner, and any partner delegation needs consent text and date (database check).
- P0.1-08: only the six cron-only functions moved to `private`. The plan's list also names `call_internal`, `partner_billing_run`, `partner_recipients`, `start_paid_plans`, `ops_emit` and the mock helpers, but Edge Functions call those through the service role over the API (which serves only public) or other functions call them by name; they stay in public with no execute for anon or authenticated, which the advisor and the suite accept. The mock helpers (`staff_mock_review`, `staff_mock_listing_edit`) are staff screens and stay browser-callable. `plan_price` is not SECURITY DEFINER and already has no execute for anon or authenticated; left in public.
- P0.1-08: `is_member`, `is_partner_member` and `is_staff` stay executable by authenticated because RLS policies call them as the signed-in user; the advisor will keep listing them, and the other 50 browser RPCs, as WARN by design.
- P0.1-08: a later migration that does `create or replace function public.run_retention()` (P0.2-01) must target `private.run_retention()` instead, or it recreates a public copy; the suite's "no copy left in public" test catches it.
- P0.1-07: the CI job applies the migrations itself in one transaction instead of letting `supabase start` apply them, so pg_cron jobs never exist long enough to call the production function URL from CI.
- P0.1-07: SECURITY DEFINER functions are tested as a signed-in stranger only; table reads are tested as all four personas. Every function checks membership before anything else, so the other personas would hit the same line.
